**Decision: inconclusive**. The R-043 branch on the D-058 incumbent: an LLM wrote bounded residual corrections (executed action = clamp(parent + clamp(D c, -0.15, 0.15), -1, 1)) in anonymous orthonormal coordinates, D the identity (joint arm) or a frozen synergy basis (anatomical arm), from one shared request; 8 slots per arm with one linked correction (31 calls, 459k tokens; 8/8 and 6/8 valid), each candidate screened on 20 frozen cases in its own process, finalists frozen (slot 7 in both arms, 9/20 and 8/20), then the finalists and the parent on the confirmation manifest's promotion (10-39) and fresh (40-119) cases (610 episodes; identity gate bit for bit on 23,484 archived observations). Predicted at least +0.05 fresh success for the anatomical finalist over the joint finalist; observed -0.025 [-0.104, +0.054] (33 vs 35 of 80; 4 wins, 6 losses, 70 ties): the interval spans zero and its upper bound is not below +0.05, so neither supported nor refuted. Neither finalist differs from the parent (fresh 35 and 33 vs 34; promotion 12 and 14 vs 14); no strict promotion, no adoption; the D-058 incumbent stands. The traces explain it: the residuals used about a tenth of their authority (mean executed-minus-parent norm 0.024 joint, 0.013 anatomical against the 0.15 bound), so both arms are the parent plus a small perturbation and the coordinate basis was never exercised; with anonymous coordinates the model cannot aim a correction and writes a cautious one. Deviation stated: candidates were screened in their own 20-environment processes rather than one tiled process per arm, the same batch shape for both arms. With this, every branch the direction named has been run: scalar tuning (D-048, D-049), the lesion panel and the curl removal that qualified and was adopted (D-050 to D-059), stroke feedback (D-058) and R-043 (this); the open problem on the incumbent is its slow-spin tail.
**Next.** Treat R-043 as answered for this procedure: with anonymous coordinates and one cohort, LLM residuals on this incumbent are too small to move anything, in either basis. If representation is tested again it needs either disclosed coordinate semantics (R-085's grounding arm, which the deferral makes conditional on an advantage that did not appear) or a residual that is forced to use its authority (a minimum-norm or screened-amplitude contract). Otherwise the D-058 incumbent stands and the slow-spin tail is the open problem; the direction's branches (tuning, repair of the winner, R-043) have all been run.

Six feedback settings (lead offset = clip(gain (target - y), +-0.6 rad), y a 0.25 s low-pass of the signed axial rate; targets 4, 6, 8 rad/s x gains +-0.10) and six constant lead offsets (+-0.1, +-0.2, +-0.4 rad) were screened with the parent on a frozen 40-case manifest (one process each), the winner per family frozen (feedback: target 6, gain +0.10; constant: +0.2 rad), and both winners and the parent scored on the confirmation manifest's promotion (10-39) and fresh (40-119) cases; identity gate bit for bit; 850 episodes, no LLM. Neither family beats the parent (fresh feedback -0.088 [-0.189, +0.014], constant -0.063 [-0.144, +0.019]; promotion 12 and 11 vs 10, neither strict); no adoption, the incumbent stands. The twelve lead settings screened flat (11-16 of 40 vs the parent's 13): the phase lead is not a lever for this controller in either form. The feedback was active (mean offset +0.39 rad, a third of steps at the clip) but its signed-rate target is ill-posed: the filtered signed rate averaged about 1 rad/s against an in-play |spin| of 4.5 because the parent's roll direction differs across cases; any future rate feedback on this learner must use an unsigned or per-case-direction rate. With D-058 (amplitude feedback), D-060 (R-043) and this, every mechanism the direction named has been run on the adopted incumbent; the stroke amplitude (D-058's fixed gain) is the one lever that moved it.

Any further feedback on this learner must use an unsigned or per-case-direction rate. **Decision: inconclusive**. Re-nominated R-076 on the D-058 incumbent: a within-cycle warp of the stroke phase, h(phi) = phi + epsilon sin(phi) on the pen's own roll angle (epsilon -0.35 or +0.35) with per-finger channels re-centred and rescaled to the unwarped cycle mean and RMS (constants frozen on a 3600-point grid), everything else unchanged; identity bit for bit at epsilon 0. Both warps and the parent screened on 40 frozen cases (warp -0.35 17/40, parent 16/40, warp +0.35 13/40), the winner frozen, winner and parent on promotion (10-39) and fresh (80-159) of the same seed-4690 manifest; 340 episodes, no LLM. Predicted at least +0.10 fresh success over the parent; observed +0.0875 [+0.015, +0.160] (32 vs 25 of 80; 8 wins, 1 loss, 71 ties; drops 3 vs 3): an improvement is established on the fresh panel but the predicted magnitude is neither supported nor refuted. Promotion +0.100 [-0.014, +0.214] (10 vs 7; 3 wins, 0 losses; drops 4 vs 4) misses the strict lower bound by 0.014, so the warp is not adopted and the incumbent stands. The negative warp slows the stroke's phase around the roll angle's zero and speeds it near +-pi; the positive warp did the opposite and screened below the parent. In-play |spin| 4.38 vs 4.04 and slow-spin tail failures 39 vs 47. This is the first stroke-shape change with an established fresh gain on the adopted incumbent and the fourth candidate on this learner whose 30-pair promotion panel landed just short of the strict rule while a fresh panel favoured it; the 90-pair instrument that qualified the clamp (D-057) is the predeclared way to settle it.
**Next.** The negative warp is the first stroke-shape change with an established fresh gain on the adopted incumbent (and the only candidate besides the fixed gain to win against it). Qualify it the way the clamp was qualified: one predeclared 90-pair instrument (three cases-10-39 panels) under the standing rule, 180 episodes, then adopt or not. If it qualifies, it becomes the incumbent and the warp magnitude could be swept open-loop (one more screen) before any further mechanism work; if not, the incumbent stands and the sequence is complete.

"bit-identical to logs/pen_spin/r081_stroke_feedback/constant_gain_fresh (80 cases), hashes verified"}.
**Decision: supported**. C1, the contact-frame decoder gate (no LLM; 800 calibration episodes on seed 4721, then 32 whole-manifest cells = 1,280 episodes on seed 4720 with constants hashed beforehand). The registered prediction, zero-request hold timeout rate no more than 0.10 below the neutral hold, is supported: 0.775 vs 0.000 (the closed reference posture retains the pen where the neutral pose never does; neither spins). The gate itself FAILED: Jacobian audit 0.004 (< 0.15), coverage 40/40 eligible in all 30 directional cells and median gain 0.526 (in [0.3, 1.5]) pass, but pooled requested-sign agreement is 0.6825 against 0.80. The failure is a request-independent relative motion of about +0.17 m/s along the normal (the pen settling and the fingers closing at steps 10-40), five times the 0.03 m/s pulse; the frozen setting does not follow the pen because every pen-following setting lost 60-90% of pairs to decoder bounds in calibration. Post hoc and not pre-declared, the paired positive-minus-negative contrast per case gives sign agreement 0.938 and gain 0.54 (thumb 0.10-0.43). The decoder is NOT qualified and R-094 (C2) stays deferred. Durable engineering findings: PhysX Jacobians and the observed fingertip linear velocity refer to each distal link's centre of mass, 15-16 mm from the link origin the observed position reports (uncorrected audit error 0.20, corrected 0.004); targets are unsmoothed; PD stiffness 1.0, damping 0.1; fingertip origins sit 4-6 cm from the pen at step 10 except the thumb.
**Next.** A second, pre-declared gate on fresh seeds: pulses after the pen has settled in the closed reference posture, pen-following on, paired positive-minus-negative contrast per case as the tracking statistic, thumb reported separately; re-triage R-094 only on a pass.

## M1: Modular vs monolithic cold generation, searched (D-065, 2026-11-20)

**Direction.** Test two representation changes for cold pen-spin generation on Stage 3, independently, native sustained success primary, final test locked: (M1) LLM-generated modular policies (MODULE_SPEC + named methods + generated composition inside GeneratedPolicy, no role hints) vs monolithic, same prompt otherwise, 24-draw search for both arms; (C1) a no-LLM contact-frame decoder gate (capsule frames, fingertip Jacobians, requested vs observed local motion); (C2, only if C1 passes) LLM contact-frame velocity rules vs direct joint generation at matched budget. No BC, RL, curriculum, or gradient fitting. One protocol at a time on the box.
**Idea R-092.** Explicit, contract-validated decomposition chosen by the LLM makes cold-generated Stage-3 controllers more behaviorally effective after matched rollout search than monolithic generation.
**Prediction.** paired_success_difference increase by at least 0.05 against baseline: monolithic arm with identical generation and 24-draw search budgets.
**Protocol.** `gpu run 'nohup bash -lc "source .venv-metaworld/bin/activate && python -u scripts/run_full_evolution.py --config config/pen_spin_plateau_repair.yaml --study modular_cold" > logs/r092_run.log 2>&1 &'` at git `cf3201affd`.

"object_geometry", "retention", "roll_drive"]]}.
**Decision: refuted**. M1: contract-validated modular vs monolithic cold generation on Stage 3, both arms searched (8 blocks, seeds 4710-4717; 4 slots per arm; the best screened slot gets the 24-candidate probe search; promotion against the scored neutral-pose null; fresh; 6,080 episodes, 82 LLM calls, 1.22 M tokens, 2 h 44 min). The modular arm's prompt adds only a MODULE_SPEC requirement with no role hints, enforced by an AST contract (observation reads inside declared modules, forward composes module outputs, an observation-to-action bypass is a validation failure). Predicted at least +0.05 fresh final-incumbent success; observed +0.0083 [-0.011, +0.028]: the upper bound is below +0.05, so the predicted magnitude is refuted, and no improvement is established. One modular promotion (block 1: 4/30 vs the null's 0/30, fresh 2/30), none monolithic. Secondaries: searched winner on fresh +0.025 [-0.011, +0.061] (inconclusive), final-window hit fraction -0.029 [-0.123, +0.065], probe-success candidates +0.125 per block [-1.6, +1.9], winner's drops -3.9 per block [-10.0, +2.3], 0 of 64 candidates with a screen success, 32/32 slots valid within one correction in both arms (the contract cost no validity). Cold Stage-3 generation is at its floor for both representations. Post hoc: left free to decompose, the model wrote state-reader modules plus a retention and a rolling module in most selected controllers, the split R-049 had hinted.
**Next.** Do not run modular conditions C and D on cold generation; test the modular format where it could matter, diagnosis and repair of a working controller. The contact branch continues with a settled-window paired-contrast decoder gate.

## C1b: settled-window paired-contrast contact decoder gate (D-066, 2026-11-20)

**Direction.** Test two representation changes for cold pen-spin generation on Stage 3, independently, native sustained success primary, final test locked: (M1) LLM-generated modular policies (MODULE_SPEC + named methods + generated composition inside GeneratedPolicy, no role hints) vs monolithic, same prompt otherwise, 24-draw search for both arms; (C1) a no-LLM contact-frame decoder gate (capsule frames, fingertip Jacobians, requested vs observed local motion); (C2, only if C1 passes) LLM contact-frame velocity rules vs direct joint generation at matched budget. No BC, RL, curriculum, or gradient fitting. One protocol at a time on the box.

**Decision: supported**. C1b, the settled-window paired-contrast contact decoder gate (no LLM; 400 calibration episodes on seed 4723, then 32 whole-manifest cells = 1,280 episodes on seed 4722 with the frozen block hashed beforehand). R-093's controller pulsed at steps 150-180, after the pen has come to rest in the closed reference posture, with pen-following on the commanded fingertip, and scored per case by the paired contrast of the positive and negative pulse. The registered retention prediction is supported (0.725 vs 0.025, +0.70 against a -0.10 margin). The gate is INCONCLUSIVE on coverage alone: two of 15 fingertip-axis contrasts have fewer than 20 eligible pairs (lf_normal 16, th_normal 19) because only 29 of 40 cases still hold the pen at step 180. Every measured check passes: Jacobian audit 0.0047, pooled paired sign agreement 1.000 over 394 pairs, pooled median paired gain 0.62, all 15 contrasts at sign agreement 1.00 with gains 0.38-0.94 (normal lowest), thumb 0.61; unpaired sign agreement 0.999. By the declared rule the decoder is not qualified and R-094 stays deferred.
**Next.** Replicate once with the identical frozen block (hash 7219ec6d...), no calibration, on a fresh 80-case manifest with every threshold unchanged, including the absolute minimum of 20 eligible pairs per contrast. A pass qualifies that exact decoder for R-094; anything else ends the contact branch under this direction.

**Decision: refuted**. C2: LLM contact-frame velocity rules vs direct joint generation on Stage 3, matched budget, with the decoder R-096 qualified (hash 7219ec6d...). 8 blocks (seeds 4730-4737), 4 slots per arm, the 24-candidate probe search, promotion against the scored neutral-pose null, fresh; both arms read the same 182-d observation with trusted per-fingertip contact features, and differ only in the output contract (20 joint targets vs 15 local relative-velocity requests decoded by the frozen controller); 6,720 episodes, 75 LLM calls, 1.16 M tokens, 3 h 23 min. Predicted at least +0.05 fresh final-incumbent success; observed 0.000 [0, 0]: no candidate of either arm was promoted in any block, so the predicted magnitude is refuted. Searched winner on fresh -0.0125 [-0.054, +0.029]; final-window hit fraction -0.034 [-0.128, +0.060]. Before selection the contact arm looks better (zero-shot screen final-window hit fraction +0.188 [+0.096, +0.281]; probe-search candidates with a success +0.875 per block [+0.34, +1.41], 11 vs 4), and none of it survives to promotion or fresh. The decoder was outside its qualified regime throughout: residual 0.108 m/s (p95 0.48) against a 0.05 m/s request cap, a bound active on 97% of in-play steps, fingertip origins 4 cm from the pen on average; the hand-authored contact controller through the same decoder scored 0.020 over 640 episodes. What is refuted is this decoder as an output contract for cold generation, not contact coordinates as such; validity was 32/32 in both arms.
**Next.** Stop the contact-output branch under this direction. If contact coordinates are pursued: qualify a decoder in the regime rollouts visit (pen moving, fingertips near the surface, bounds inactive), or test the contact features as observations only against a paired no-feature control.

## I1: Reproduce the incumbent and gate decoder control during rolling (D-069, 2026-11-21)

**Direction.** No LLM calls in any protocol. Test bounded residual repairs of the working fixed-stroke incumbent (config/pen_spin/d058_fixed_stroke_incumbent) on Stage 3, in four separately gated steps: (S1) reproduce the incumbent on fixed cases and quantify its slow-spin failures, with angular progress measured along each case's own roll direction; (S2) qualify the contact-frame decoder DURING ROLLING on top of the incumbent stroke, reporting requested, feasible (after bounds and least squares) and achieved motion separately, with tracking error, directional agreement and bound activity as pass/fail criteria (controllability gate); (S3) only if S2 passes, compare incumbent alone, a hand-authored contact-frame residual and a hand-authored anonymous-coordinate joint residual, with matched tuning budgets (same parameter count, same number of probe evaluations, same tuning method, tuned on probe cases and frozen before any fresh case), candidates ranked by tail-hit fraction with sustained unsigned angular progress and retention inspected; (S4) evaluate the selected controllers on fresh paired cases, 90 pairs per comparison, Holm correction over the two comparisons, promotion requires the strict paired sustained-success rule AND a pre-declared numeric non-inferiority bound on drops (usefulness gate). Residuals must not assume a roll sign. If S2 fails, the anonymous residual may still be compared against the incumbent alone. A failed hand-authored controller shelves that decoder/controller family only. No BC, RL, curriculum or gradient fitting. One protocol at a time on the box.
**Idea R-097.** During the incumbent's rolling stroke, small bounded contact-frame joint increments added to the incumbent's action produce directionally reliable relative fingertip motion (paired + / - contrast). This is about directional tracking only; retention under a nonzero pulse is reported per cell and is not claimed.
**Prediction.** gate_statistics increase by at least 0.3 against baseline: 0.50 sign-balanced chance agreement.

**Decision: refuted**. I1 (S1 + S2 of the no-LLM incumbent-residual direction): reproduce the D-058 fixed-stroke incumbent, then gate contact-frame decoder control during rolling (rolling_decoder_gate.py; 80 + 800 + 2,480 = 3,360 episodes on seeds 4741 / 4740, no LLM, about 35 minutes; gate 0 replay bit-identical). S1 reproduces: 28/80 = 0.350 sustained success, Wilson 95% [0.255, 0.459] overlapping the recorded [0.35, 0.44]; failures are 42 slow spin, 8 early drops (median fall step 32) and 2 off-centre; slow-spin cases roll steadily in one direction at about a third of the successes' rate (median 1.68 vs 5.61 rad/s, one reversal each), so the failure is low rate, not direction instability; 57 cases roll positive and 15 negative. S2: a 0.02 m/s contact-frame pulse at steps 150-180, decoded into an integrating joint increment ADDED to the incumbent's action, scored as the paired +/- contrast per case. The gate FAILS and the registered prediction is refuted: pooled paired sign agreement 0.724 over 840 pairs, case-clustered 95% interval [0.710, 0.739] against 0.80; weakest contrast 0.036 (minimum 0.70); feasible/requested 0.445 (minimum 0.70); a bound active on 0.44 of pulse steps (maximum 0.20); tracking error 0.84 (maximum 0.70); pooled gain 0.34 (in range). Every instrument check passes: Jacobian audit 0.0025, zero-request residual identical to the incumbent (0.0), in-play prefixes bit-identical between processes, 56 eligible pairs in each of 15 contrasts. No calibration setting met the bound and feasibility thresholds (bound activity 0.39-0.59), so the declared fallback froze cal2 (damping 1e-3, cap 0.15 rad). Post hoc, not pre-declared: the split is by finger. Forefinger and ring finger track (agreement 0.994 and 1.000, bound activity 0.05 and 0.14); middle finger 0.411 and thumb 0.315 do not, little finger 0.899 with bounds active on 0.71 of steps. The incumbent's own action is saturated at +/-1 on MFJ2 on 79% and LFJ1 on 64% of pulse steps, so an added increment is clipped there, and for mf_circumferential the pen surface moves with the request more than the fingertip does (that finger carries the pen). Pulses changed no episode length and sustained successes stayed at 25-30 against 28. Scope: this decoder as an additive residual on this incumbent at this pulse; it is not qualified for R-099's contact arm. It does not show contact coordinates cannot help. One analysis fix before the gate ran: the prefix-identity check now compares in-play steps only (post-termination auto-reset steps had been included).
**Next.** Do not run the contact arm of R-099. Re-triage R-099 as its declared anonymous-only fallback against the incumbent (2,180 episodes), which needs no decoder. If contact coordinates are pursued later: restrict to the two fingers that track (ff, rf), or give the residual authority over the incumbent's saturated targets rather than adding to a clipped action.

## I2 (anonymous-only fallback): bounded joint-motion residual on the incumbent, searched then qualified on fresh pairs (D-070, 2026-11-21)

**Direction.** No LLM calls in any protocol. Test bounded residual repairs of the working fixed-stroke incumbent (config/pen_spin/d058_fixed_stroke_incumbent) on Stage 3, in four separately gated steps: (S1) reproduce the incumbent on fixed cases and quantify its slow-spin failures, with angular progress measured along each case's own roll direction; (S2) qualify the contact-frame decoder DURING ROLLING on top of the incumbent stroke, reporting requested, feasible (after bounds and least squares) and achieved motion separately, with tracking error, directional agreement and bound activity as pass/fail criteria (controllability gate); (S3) only if S2 passes, compare incumbent alone, a hand-authored contact-frame residual and a hand-authored anonymous-coordinate joint residual, with matched tuning budgets (same parameter count, same number of probe evaluations, same tuning method, tuned on probe cases and frozen before any fresh case), candidates ranked by tail-hit fraction with sustained unsigned angular progress and retention inspected; (S4) evaluate the selected controllers on fresh paired cases, 90 pairs per comparison, Holm correction over the two comparisons, promotion requires the strict paired sustained-success rule AND a pre-declared numeric non-inferiority bound on drops (usefulness gate). Residuals must not assume a roll sign. If S2 fails, the anonymous residual may still be compared against the incumbent alone. A failed hand-authored controller shelves that decoder/controller family only. No BC, RL, curriculum or gradient fitting. One protocol at a time on the box.
**Idea R-099.** Bounded reinforcement of filtered joint motion sustains effective movement through slow-spin tails without increasing drops. (The original contact-residual prediction is UNTESTED: R-097 / D-069 failed the rolling decoder gate, so the contact arm is not run.)
**Prediction.** paired_success_difference increase by at least 0.1 against the unchanged D-058 fixed-stroke incumbent on the same 90 fresh paired cases (seed 4744).
**Protocol.** `python scripts/run_shadowhand_spin_stages.py --study incumbent_residual` at git `eb1f08a298`.

**Decision: inconclusive**. I2 (S3 + S4 of the no-LLM incumbent-residual direction), anonymous-only fallback after D-069: a bounded joint-motion residual on the D-058 incumbent (incumbent_residual.py; delta = clip(g sign(v_f) max(|v_f| - v0, 0), -b, b) on the 20 actuated joints' filtered velocities, four scalars, no fingertip or pen geometry, no roll sign; 2,000 + 180 = 2,180 episodes, no LLM, about 20 minutes). The contact arm was not run and its prediction is untested. S3 (seed 4742, 80 probe cases): 24 seeded draws; incumbent 31/80, tail-hit 0.416, 3 drops; draws 22-39 successes (median 33, 14 above the incumbent), 13 draws with zero drops, 22 of 24 raising unsigned roll; the low-gain draws lead and the largest-gain draws are worst. Winner by the declared ranking, frozen and hashed (2a509ef5...) before any fresh case: draw7 (gain 0.0047 s, deadband 0.23 rad/s, tau 0.185 s, cap 0.103 rad), 39/80, tail-hit 0.496, 0 drops. S4 (seed 4744, 90 fresh pairs): 41/90 vs the incumbent's 36/90, paired sustained-success difference +0.056, 95% interval [-0.041, +0.152] (12 wins, 7 losses, 71 ties); drops 1 vs 1 (one-sided 95% upper bound 0.0 <= 0.05). INCONCLUSIVE against the predicted +0.10: the interval contains both zero and +0.10. The residual does not promote and is not adopted; no improvement is established. Secondaries, not the registered metric: unsigned roll 28.0 vs 22.0 rad, mean |axial rate| 4.90 vs 3.89 rad/s, slow-spin failures 44 vs 48, tail-hit +0.048 [-0.046, +0.142], with a mean residual of 0.0066 rad. The 90-pair instrument resolves about +/-0.10 (paired SE 0.048), so an effect near +0.05 is below what it can decide. Scope: this joint-motion-reinforcement family, this incumbent, one 24-draw search. One deviation: the first S4 invocation crashed after the incumbent's cell and before the residual's ran (the driver's frozen-winner record shared the filename of the residual's cell); fixed and resumed with nothing retuned and no fresh residual result seen beforehand.
**Next.** Do not adopt. The cheapest decisive step is a prospective replication of the SAME frozen residual (hash 2a509ef5...) on a larger fresh paired instrument sized for +0.05 (about 360 pairs for SE 0.024), with no retuning, as D-057 did for the curl clamp; otherwise shelve this family. R-098 stays deferred (its precondition, a passing decoder gate, did not occur).

## Case-directed ff/rf fingertip-to-pen transfer audit (D-071, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** No LLM calls in any protocol. Two finite follow-ups to D-069 and D-070 on Stage 3, on the fixed-stroke incumbent (config/pen_spin/d058_fixed_stroke_incumbent). (A) A transfer audit: on about 40 fresh cases, run the incumbent alone and with forefinger/ring circumferential pulses along and against each case's own roll direction, logging clamp activity, joint motion, fingertip and pen-surface velocity, and contact impulses; the prediction is on paired roll progress, pro minus anti. (B) One confirmatory replication of D-070's frozen residual, unchanged, on about 670 fresh pairs against the incumbent, with the strict paired success rule and the drop bound. No retuning, no new controller search, no further replication after (B). One protocol at a time on the box.
**Idea R-101.** Coherent forefinger and ring-finger circumferential motion transfers to pen roll, producing more progress when aligned with each case's existing roll than when opposed.
**Prediction.** signed_goal_progress increase by at least 0.1 against same-manifest anti-direction pulse.
**Protocol.** `python scripts/run_shadowhand_spin_stages.py --study transfer_audit` at git `a6bd76f807`.

**Decision: refuted**. Refuted: with adequate forefinger/ring authority (0.69 and 0.82 of full displacement), coherent circumferential pulses changed pen roll by -0.054 rad [-0.147, +0.038] pro minus anti, below the predicted +0.10. Post hoc: those fingertips are about 58 mm from the pen under this incumbent; only the middle fingertip and thumb are at the surface.
**Next.** Run R-102 as agreed. Any further contact-interface work on this incumbent has to act on the middle finger and thumb, which D-069 showed do not track and which are partly saturated; that is a new direction, not a follow-up under this one.

## Final 672-pair confirmation of D-070's frozen residual (D-072, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** No LLM calls in any protocol. Two finite follow-ups to D-069 and D-070 on Stage 3, on the fixed-stroke incumbent (config/pen_spin/d058_fixed_stroke_incumbent). (A) A transfer audit: on about 40 fresh cases, run the incumbent alone and with forefinger/ring circumferential pulses along and against each case's own roll direction, logging clamp activity, joint motion, fingertip and pen-surface velocity, and contact impulses; the prediction is on paired roll progress, pro minus anti. (B) One confirmatory replication of D-070's frozen residual, unchanged, on about 670 fresh pairs against the incumbent, with the strict paired success rule and the drop bound. No retuning, no new controller search, no further replication after (B). One protocol at a time on the box.
**Idea R-102.** The unchanged low-gain joint-velocity residual reinforces existing joint motion without assuming a roll sign, converting slow-spin failures into sustained successes without materially increasing drops.
**Prediction.** paired_success_difference increase by at least 0.05 against config/pen_spin/d058_fixed_stroke_incumbent.
**Protocol.** `python scripts/run_shadowhand_spin_stages.py --study incumbent_residual_confirmation` at git `a6bd76f807`.

## P1: Reward-trained symbolic residuals on the qualified reference (D-073, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** Continue Stage-3 Shadow Hand axial rolling by testing reward-trained symbolic residuals and incremental policy construction. Use the frozen D-072-qualified controller as the experimental reference, without changing the saved default incumbent. First compare independent parameter draws, ARS/CEM and PPO on one identical sparse, state-conditioned symbolic residual family. If an inner learner produces a confirmed useful gain, compare fixed structure, grammar-based structural evolution and LLM-guided structural evolution with that learner and inherited parameters. Investigate compact memory or anchored component replacement only when the preceding results motivate them. Preserve the task, frozen evaluation manifests and strict paired promotion/drop criteria. Run one protocol at a time. No controller-generation LLM calls during P1; advisor calls for the research loop remain allowed. Later controller-generation calls need an agreed protocol and explicit call budget. Do not repeat R-102, retune its confirmation cases, reopen cold whole-controller generation, or resume expert cloning. Continue evidence-driven follow-ups within this direction through the existing loop, including its escalation and exhaustion rules.
**Idea R-103.** Action-level reward credit assignment will learn sparse state-conditioned corrections to the frozen D-072-qualified controller that improve sustained axial-rolling success more than independent parameter draws.
**Prediction.** paired_success_difference increase by at least 0.05 against the independent-parameter-draw arm's finalists, matched by training block and fresh case (384 pairs).
**Protocol.** `python scripts/run_shadowhand_spin_stages.py --study symbolic_reward_residual` at git `2d0907908e`.

**Decision: inconclusive**. P1 (incomplete: two of three blocks): one 32-coefficient symbolic residual on the D-072-qualified reference (slow-spin-gated posture offsets, group gains on filtered joint velocity and posture error; bound and cap 0.10; symbolic_reward.py), trained from one dense reward by independent draws, ARS and PPO (latent-Gaussian likelihood, neural critic), each at exactly 460,800 transitions per run with 128 environments; checkpoints selected on 64 cases per block, finalists frozen, then 128 fresh cases per block. Registered contrast, PPO minus draws on fresh: +0.004, case-paired 95% interval [-0.059, +0.067] over 256 pairs (34 wins, 33 losses), Holm p 1.0: INCONCLUSIVE (contains zero and +0.05). No trained arm beats the reference: PPO -0.043 [-0.103, +0.017], ARS +0.004 [-0.048, +0.056] (one finalist is the reference, training rejected), draws -0.047 [-0.103, +0.010]; ARS minus draws +0.051 [-0.015, +0.116]; no Holm-adjusted p below 0.62; drop bounds all within 0.05. Instrument checks passed on the rerun smoke (zero-theta identity, per-environment filter reset, executed/proposed residual 0.99, likelihood ratio exactly 1, checkpoint reconstruction), so authority is not the limit: residuals execute and do not help. Selection on 64 cases did not carry over (b0 PPO 38/64 vs 32/64 selected, 58/128 vs 59/128 fresh); ARS and PPO drive coefficients to the bound. Two deviations: the first smoke (seed 4763) failed on a defect in the implementer's joint-order check (fixed; rerun once on seed 4770 by the user's decision); training stopped on the hard 20,000-episode ceiling at the seventh run because the implementer under-counted starts per run (about 2,563 for draws/ARS), so block 2 is void and blocks 0-1 were evaluated under a recorded amendment. 17,926 episodes charged, 0 LLM calls. Scope: two runs per learner at this budget; not evidence that reward learning cannot work in this family. P2 does not open.
**Next.** Do not extend R-103 and do not open P2 (no learner has a confirmed gain). The cheapest informative follow-up inside the direction is diagnostic, not more of the same: the selection instrument (64 cases) cannot rank checkpoints whose true differences are a few points, and both adaptive learners saturate the coefficient bound within a third of the budget while fresh success falls slightly, which points at objective mismatch (dense reward vs sustained success) or too-large steps rather than authority. Options for the advisor: (a) an offline/cheap audit of reward-versus-success alignment using the recorded training curves and the fresh cells already on disk; (b) a preregistered rerun with corrected episode accounting, a larger selection instrument and a smaller step/bound; (c) P4-style anchored component replacement if (a) says whole-residual learning is objective-limited.

## Audit whether a late-tail score ranks sustained success better than accumulated dense rolling (archive only) (D-074, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** Continue Stage-3 Shadow Hand axial rolling by testing reward-trained symbolic residuals and incremental policy construction. Use the frozen D-072-qualified controller as the experimental reference, without changing the saved default incumbent. First compare independent parameter draws, ARS/CEM and PPO on one identical sparse, state-conditioned symbolic residual family. If an inner learner produces a confirmed useful gain, compare fixed structure, grammar-based structural evolution and LLM-guided structural evolution with that learner and inherited parameters. Investigate compact memory or anchored component replacement only when the preceding results motivate them. Preserve the task, frozen evaluation manifests and strict paired promotion/drop criteria. Run one protocol at a time. No controller-generation LLM calls during P1; advisor calls for the research loop remain allowed. Later controller-generation calls need an agreed protocol and explicit call budget. Do not repeat R-102, retune its confirmation cases, reopen cold whole-controller generation, or resume expert cloning. Continue evidence-driven follow-ups within this direction through the existing loop, including its escalation and exhaustion rules.

**Decision: refuted**. Archive-only audit of R-103's fresh cells (symbolic_reward_audit.py; no simulator, no LLM): does a late-tail score (360 x mean of the six smallest smooth rolling terms in steps 300-359, minus 5 on a fall) rank sustained success better than the accumulated dense score, on success-discordant case comparisons among the reference and six distinct finalists? REFUTED: concordance(tail) - concordance(dense) = +0.028, case-clustered bootstrap 95% [+0.009, +0.053] over 330 discordant comparisons against a predicted +0.10. The dense score is already concordant on 0.972 [0.947, 0.991]; the tail score is 1.000 because it nearly restates the success rule. Both scores omit the residual penalty (not reconstructable; 1.8% of dense margins lie within its bound). Not the registered statistic: between policies, mean score and success are not ordered together (block 1 PPO has a higher mean dense score than the reference, 118.6 vs 116.9, and 7.8 points lower success), so a learner can raise average reward through episodes that still fail. Sizing (planning only): two policies disagree on 0.16-0.41 of cases; a paired success difference on 64 cases has SD 0.051-0.080, so R-103's checkpoint selection could not rank policies a few points apart; +0.05 at 80% power needs about 500-1,270 pairs. One procedural deviation: the deterministic audit ran once before the ledger start succeeded; the rerun is byte-identical.
**Next.** Do not run R-105 as written: its premise (the tail score is much better aligned) is refuted at the case level, and its 256-case selection has an SD of 0.03-0.04. If a learning rerun is wanted it should change what R-104 identifies: select with far more cases (or do not select: evaluate the final checkpoint only), and optimise a per-episode success-shaped objective in parameter space (ARS on the tail score or on the success predicate count) rather than average step reward. Otherwise move to P4 (anchored component replacement) or ask whether this residual family is worth more budget.

ARS on the exact sustained-success count in R-103's 32-coefficient residual family on the D-072-qualified reference (success_ars.py; no LLM): 12 updates, 4 antithetic directions (nu 0.02, alpha 0.01, all used), each update two 2,048-environment rollouts with every signed candidate on the same 512 placements, 17.6 M training transitions in 8 minutes; the final parameter mean was frozen and hashed with no checkpoint selection, then confirmed once on twelve fresh 128-case manifests (seeds 4780-4791) through the one-manifest-per-process evaluator. SUPPORTED: finalist minus reference +0.109 paired sustained success, 95% interval [+0.079, +0.138] over 1,536 pairs (806 vs 639; 355 wins, 188 losses), manifest-clustered [+0.084, +0.134], positive in 12 of 12 manifests; drops 71 vs 83 (one-sided upper bound -0.001 against +0.05); tail-hit fraction +0.116 [+0.088, +0.145]. The strict paired rule and the drop gate both pass and the lower bound is above the predicted +0.05. Finalist: max |coefficient| 0.086, mean executed residual 0.021 action units; mostly slow-spin-gated posture offsets (thumb, middle and forefinger flexion). A/A yardstick: the same policy on the same 512 placements in four batch slots differs by at most 0.006 in success (case discordance 0.07-0.10). One deviation: the first smoke (seed 4778) failed on two defects in the implementer's child (a completed episode has 359 recorded steps, not 360; bit identity across slots was the wrong check); fixed, rerun once on seed 4792, ceiling raised to 125,000 starts before any training (120,972 charged). Scope: one ARS trajectory; the cause is not isolated (exact-success objective, 512 shared cases per candidate and 38x R-103's transitions changed together). The saved incumbent asset is unchanged; installing the finalist is the user's call. The direction's condition for P2 is met.
**Next.** This meets the direction's condition for P2 (a frozen finalist with prospectively confirmed improvement over the qualified reference that passes the drop bound). Next: propose P2 (fixed structure vs grammar edits vs LLM edits with this inner learner, inherited parameters, explicit call budget), with the R-106 finalist as the experimental reference. Whether to install the finalist as the incumbent asset is the user's call. A cheap attribution ablation (dense-reward ARS in the identical layout and budget) would say whether the objective or the instrument size mattered, but it is not needed to open P2.

## 1. Isolate exact-success ARS from dense-reward ARS (D-076, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** Continue Stage-3 Shadow Hand axial rolling by testing reward-trained symbolic residuals and incremental policy construction. Do not repeat R-102, retune its confirmation cases, reopen cold whole-controller generation, or resume expert cloning. Continue evidence-driven follow-ups within this direction through the existing loop, including its escalation and exhaustion rules.
**Idea R-108.** Exact-success ARS improves sustained success more than dense-reward ARS because it allocates parameter updates to crossing the episode-success boundary rather than increasing reward within unsuccessful episodes.

**Decision: refuted**. Objective attribution for D-075 (success_ars.run_attribution; no LLM): two ARS trajectories identical in everything (zero start on the D-072 reference, the 32-coefficient family, 12 updates x 4 antithetic directions, the same 512 shared placements per signed candidate and the same direction streams, 17.6 M transitions each) except the return optimised: the sustained-success fraction (exact) or R-103's summed dense step reward (dense). Both terminal means frozen without selection, then the reference, both finalists and the frozen R-106 finalist on twelve fresh 128-case manifests (seeds 4822-4833). REFUTED: exact minus dense -0.021 paired sustained success, 95% [-0.046, +0.005] over 1,536 pairs (793 vs 825), manifest-clustered [-0.055, +0.014], against a predicted +0.05. Both arms beat the reference and pass both adoption gates: exact +0.087 [+0.058, +0.116], dense +0.108 [+0.080, +0.136], each 12 of 12 manifests, drops 46 and 51 vs 74. The frozen R-106 finalist replicates on these new cases: +0.110 [+0.080, +0.140] (828 vs 659). The two finalists point the same way (cosine 0.84) and their training curves are indistinguishable. So D-075's gain is due to the instrument (many shared placements per candidate in one batch, 38x R-103's transitions), not the exact-success objective; with a quiet contrast the dense reward works equally well. One deviation: one evaluation process crashed at simulator start-up (malloc error) before any result; the driver resumed and ran the remaining cells. One matched pair of trajectories; 217,323 starts charged, 0 LLM calls.
**Next.** Run R-109 (P2) as approved; its inner learner (exact-success ARS in this layout) is a sound choice either way, and this result says the dense return would serve equally. For later ideas: P1's original comparison (PPO vs ARS vs draws) was confounded by the same instrument problem and could be rerun fairly now, but P2 comes first under the direction.

**Decision: inconclusive**. P2 pilot (structure_search.py, structure_terms.py; 12 LLM calls, 40k tokens, 0 invalid edits): from the frozen R-106 finalist, three blocks x two rounds; per round each arm spends 12 exact-success ARS updates and submits two candidates: fixed (continued fitting of the 32-coefficient structure), grammar (parent plus one sampled term from a typed edit language over observation primitives), llm (parent plus one model-proposed term in the same language). Finalists frozen, then the D-072 reference, the R-106 root and the three finalists on twelve fresh 128-case manifests per block. INCONCLUSIVE on the registered statistic: LLM minus grammar +0.009, nominal three-block interval [-0.047, +0.064] (blocks +0.034, -0.009, +0.002); pooled case-paired over 4,608 pairs [-0.006, +0.024]. No structural arm beats budget-matched continued fitting (grammar - fixed -0.010 [-0.025, +0.005]; LLM - fixed -0.001 [-0.016, +0.013]) and no arm improves on the R-106 root (fixed +0.009 [-0.006, +0.025], LLM +0.008, grammar -0.001); all trained policies stay +0.12 to +0.13 above the D-072 reference in every block (root +0.125 [+0.107, +0.142], a second replication of D-075). No structural-evolution contribution is claimed; the fixed structure's own gains have flattened. Two caveats that make the across-block intervals nominal: an OUTCOME-INFORMED amendment after the first two selection decisions replaced the registered strict Holm gate (under which all 18 selections would have kept the parent and every contrast would have been exactly 0) with 'best success within the drop bound' (13 of 18 decisions then moved); and a seed-stream defect in the frozen spec made neighbouring blocks' grammar arms share one edit per round. 2,043,223 simulator starts charged.
**Next.** P2 as a three-block pilot is done: neither grammar nor LLM edits add value over continued fitting at this budget, and continued fitting itself has flattened, so the binding constraint is no longer structure or optimisation budget in this residual-on-reference form. Options for the advisor: (a) stop structural search in this family and report; (b) P4, anchored component replacement, which lets the learner change the reference's own stroke/feedback coefficients instead of adding a bounded residual (the residual is capped at 0.10 and three finalists sit near the bound); (c) a failure-mode audit of the R-106 finalist on archived cells (which failures remain: slow spin vs drops vs off-centre) to decide where capacity is needed before spending more. If any further structure study is run, fix the seed-stream collision and use a selection rule sized to the per-round gain.

**Decision: refuted**. Archive-only audit (slow_mode_audit.py; no simulator, no LLM) of the R-106 finalist's 4,608 archived confirmation episodes from R-109: can a class-balanced L2 logistic gate on the 29 reset placement draws and their squares, held out by manifest group, separate slow-spin failures (1,904) from sustained successes (2,507)? REFUTED at the registered strength: held-out balanced accuracy 0.608, advantage +0.108 over the constant control, manifest-bootstrap 95% [+0.095, +0.120], against a predicted +0.15 (a real but weak dependence; mostly the pen's y position and second rotation draw). Diagnostics, not registered: the mode is largely settled in the first second (prefix gates 0.752 at 30 steps and 0.811 at 60, AUC 0.90; no separation at step 10); it is not a property of the case (of the reference's 1,932 successes on the same cases the finalist is slow on 574, and 1,071 of the reference's 2,271 slow cases become finalist successes); episodes rolling in the negative direction (689) succeed at 0.32 against 0.61 for the positive direction (3,722). Reading: the slow mode is an early, sensitive capture outcome that then persists for five seconds; a memoryless controller cannot distinguish 'starting' from 'stuck'. R-111 (posture-then-stroke native fitting, conditional on a reset dependence) stays deferred.
**Next.** Do not run R-111 as written (posture-then-stroke fitting was conditional on a reset/capture-posture dependence; the dependence found is weak). The evidence points to (i) the first 60 steps (capture transient) and (ii) episodes that, once slow, stay slow for the remaining five seconds: a controller with no memory cannot tell 'still starting' from 'stuck', so P3 (compact memory) is now motivated by traces: e.g. a slow-for-N-steps latch that triggers a re-start of the capture/stroke transient, compared against the same term without memory. Alternatively a roll-direction-aware term (the reference and its residual treat both directions alike, and the negative direction is twice as likely to fail). Ask the advisor to choose.

"4556880d927f962151c165120860666850e663bb3ed42c08c5c8a6539c988cfc"}.
**Decision: refuted**. P3 pilot (memory_study.py; no LLM): three arms from the frozen R-106 finalist, each adding the same 26 zero-initialised coefficients (20 gated posture offsets, 6 gated reference-action gains), the gate being the instantaneous spin deficit (INSTANT), the same through the reference's ~11-step filter (SINGLE_FILTER), or a 60-step slow memory that is 0 at reset (TWO_TIMESCALE); same placements and ARS direction streams, 12 exact-success ARS updates, terminal means frozen, then the reference, the root and the three finalists on twelve fresh 128-case manifests. REFUTED: TWO_TIMESCALE minus SINGLE_FILTER -0.001, 95% [-0.029, +0.027] over 1,536 pairs (manifest-clustered [-0.031, +0.029]) against a predicted +0.05. SINGLE_FILTER minus INSTANT -0.008 [-0.035, +0.019]. No arm improves on the root (instant +0.012, single filter +0.004, two-timescale +0.003; all intervals within [-0.024, +0.039]; Holm p 1.0) although each moved its new coefficients (posture offsets up to 0.06-0.07). Root minus reference +0.110 [+0.079, +0.141], the fourth independent confirmation of D-075. Smoke passed first time (bitwise inheritance, activation, per-environment state reset; the slow state spans its range). No deviation; 318,673 starts charged. Together with D-077: more updates, structural terms and memory all leave the bounded residual at about 0.54 success; the 0.10-capped residual-on-reference form looks saturated.
**Next.** Three different additions to the bounded residual have now failed to move the root (more updates, structural terms, memory): the residual-on-reference form with a 0.10 cap looks saturated at about 0.54 success. What is untested is authority, not information: whether ANY intervention gets a stuck episode out of the slow mode. Before more learning, run the cheap scripted diagnostic: in episodes that are slow at a fixed step, apply a scripted re-capture (for example return to the reset grasp posture for N steps, or a larger-amplitude stroke) and measure the conversion rate against untouched stuck episodes on the same cases; if nothing scripted converts them, the slow mode needs a different controller (P4 on the reference's own stroke), and if something does, learn its trigger. R-113 (direction-odd term) is another bounded residual term and should stay deferred.

## 1. Establish recovery authority with three frozen interventions (D-080, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** Continue Stage-3 Shadow Hand axial rolling by testing reward-trained symbolic residuals and incremental policy construction. Use the frozen D-072-qualified controller as the experimental reference, without changing the saved default incumbent. First compare independent parameter draws, ARS/CEM and PPO on one identical sparse, state-conditioned symbolic residual family. If an inner learner produces a confirmed useful gain, compare fixed structure, grammar-based structural evolution and LLM-guided structural evolution with that learner and inherited parameters. Investigate compact memory or anchored component replacement only when the preceding results motivate them. Preserve the task, frozen evaluation manifests and strict paired promotion/drop criteria. Run one protocol at a time. No controller-generation LLM calls during P1; advisor calls for the research loop remain allowed. Later controller-generation calls need an agreed protocol and explicit call budget. Do not repeat R-102, retune its confirmation cases, reopen cold whole-controller generation, or resume expert cloning. Continue evidence-driven follow-ups within this direction through the existing loop, including its escalation and exhaustion rules.
**Idea R-114.** Briefly reopening and recatching the grasp can move a persistently slow episode into a rolling contact configuration that the frozen R-106 finalist can sustain.
**Prediction.** paired_success_difference increase by at least 0.05 against R-106 finalist with no intervention, restricted to placement pairs stuck before intervention.
**Protocol.** `python scripts/run_shadowhand_spin_stages.py --study recovery_authority` at git `16eb622eb3`.

**Decision: supported**. Scripted recovery diagnostic on the frozen R-106 finalist (recovery_authority.py; no learning, no LLM; in-process shared-placement rollouts, four batches of 4 x 512, 16,394 starts): episodes that are alive, centred and slower than 3 rad/s at step 120 (30.9% of 2,048; untreated they recover in 0.8% of cases) receive for 60 steps nothing, a re-capture blend toward the hand's reset posture and back, a 1.5x stroke gain, or the residual x3 with a 0.30 cap. SUPPORTED: recapture minus control +0.107 paired sustained success among jointly stuck pairs, 95% [+0.079, +0.134] over 525 pairs (58 wins, 2 losses), Holm p < 0.001, batch-clustered [+0.052, +0.169]: escape authority exists. It is not yet a usable intervention: the drop bound fails (paired drop difference +0.084, upper bound +0.104 against +0.05; 59 of 633 treated episodes fall, median step 159). Post hoc: 59% of the treated survivors end rolling above 4 rad/s on average (median 5.6, untreated 1.3%) but only 12% of treated episodes meet the strict sustained rule, so retention during the manoeuvre and steadiness afterwards are what is missing. Stroke boost: +0.019 [+0.007, +0.031], no drops (small, safe, below the registered +0.05). Residual x3: +0.006 [-0.003, +0.014]: the residual cap is not what binds. No deviation; instrument checks passed (control success 0.542).
**Next.** Authority exists, so the problem is now the SHAPE and TRIGGER of the re-capture: keep the pen during the manoeuvre (59 of 633 treated episodes fall) and make the restarted roll steady and centred (59% of survivors roll above 4 rad/s on average but only 12% meet the strict rule). Next: learn a parameterised recovery behaviour with the instrument that works (ARS on shared placements): a small symbolic recovery module on top of the frozen R-106 finalist (trigger threshold and timing, blend depth, ramp durations, a per-group blend instead of all joints, optionally a stroke boost after re-catch), trained on the full-episode success count so that drops are penalised by the objective itself, then confirmed on fresh manifests against the root with the strict rule and the drop bound. That is the direction's anchored incremental construction (P4) applied where authority was shown, and it supersedes R-115 / R-111.

**Decision: supported**. Learned symbolic re-capture module on the frozen R-106 finalist (recovery_module.py; no LLM): 11 bounded parameters starting at the D-080 script (trigger threshold, decision step, ramp-in/out, blend depth per joint group toward the reset posture, post-catch stroke multiplier), one decision per episode from the observation only, trained by ARS in normalised coordinates on the full-episode sustained-success count (12 updates, 512 shared placements per signed candidate, 98,458 starts); terminal mean frozen, then the D-072 reference, the root, the untrained script and the trained module on twelve fresh 128-case manifests (seeds 4942-4953). SUPPORTED: trained minus root +0.082 paired sustained success, 95% [+0.068, +0.096] over 1,536 pairs (953 vs 827; 130 wins, 4 losses), manifest-clustered [+0.069, +0.095], 12 of 12 manifests; paired drop difference +0.014, upper bound +0.019 (bound holds). Trained minus the untrained script +0.051 [+0.037, +0.066] with fewer drops (68 vs 85): the gain is credited to learning. Script minus root +0.031 [+0.021, +0.040]. Trained minus reference +0.179 [+0.147, +0.211] (953 vs 678), drops 68 vs 81. Root minus reference +0.097 (fifth confirmation of D-075). What learning changed: the thumb opens less than half way (blend depth 1.00 -> 0.42; little finger 0.69, wrist 0.79), the decision moves from step 120 to 97, threshold 3.0 -> 2.64 rad/s; the stroke multiplier stayed at 1.0. Smoke passed first time; no deviation; 118,942 starts charged. One ARS trajectory, not converged at update 12. The saved incumbent asset is unchanged; the module is frozen in config/pen_spin/r116_recovery_finalist/.
**Next.** This is a qualified improvement on the R-106 finalist (about 0.62 sustained success against 0.54 and the reference's 0.44); installing it as the incumbent asset is the user's call. Within the direction: (1) the module was not converged, so a budget-matched continuation (more updates of the same 11 parameters) is the cheap next step and the right control for anything structural; (2) R-117 (event-terminated support) had preconditions that are NOT met (the stroke multiplier stayed at 1.0), so it should be cancelled or re-triaged; (3) the remaining failures should be re-audited on this confirmation's archive (how many triggered episodes still end slow, how many never trigger, how many drop during re-capture) before choosing between a second attempt, a trigger on the early window (steps 30-60, where D-078 says the mode is already predictable) and joint co-training of the residual with the module.

## 1. Repeated re-capture versus budget-matched single-attempt continuation (D-082, 2026-11-22)

**Direction.** Continue Stage-3 Shadow Hand axial rolling by testing reward-trained symbolic residuals and incremental policy construction. Use the frozen D-072-qualified controller as the experimental reference, without changing the saved default incumbent. First compare independent parameter draws, ARS/CEM and PPO on one identical sparse, state-conditioned symbolic residual family. If an inner learner produces a confirmed useful gain, compare fixed structure, grammar-based structural evolution and LLM-guided structural evolution with that learner and inherited parameters. Investigate compact memory or anchored component replacement only when the preceding results motivate them. Preserve the task, frozen evaluation manifests and strict paired promotion/drop criteria. Run one protocol at a time. No controller-generation LLM calls during P1; advisor calls for the research loop remain allowed. Later controller-generation calls need an agreed protocol and explicit call budget. Do not repeat R-102, retune its confirmation cases, reopen cold whole-controller generation, or resume expert cloning. Continue evidence-driven follow-ups within this direction through the existing loop, including its escalation and exhaustion rules.
**Idea R-118.** A second state-gated re-capture can escape persistent slow states that survive one attempt, beyond gains obtainable by continuing optimization of the original module.
**Prediction.** paired_success_difference increase by at least 0.05 against CONTINUE: budget-matched optimization of the inherited 11-parameter single-attempt module.
**Protocol.** `python scripts/run_shadowhand_spin_stages.py --study recovery_retry_vs_continue` at git `bb25e17036`.

**Decision: refuted**. From the frozen R-116 re-capture module, two ARS arms of 24 updates on the same placements with shared direction draws (recovery_retry.py; no LLM): CONTINUE (the same 11 parameters, one decision) and RETRY (the same plus a gap and a second-attempt depth, initially 0 = the parent bit for bit). Terminal means frozen, then the D-072 reference, the R-106 root, the R-116 module and both finalists on twelve fresh 128-case manifests (seeds 4971-4982). REFUTED: RETRY minus CONTINUE +0.004, 95% [-0.009, +0.017] over 1,536 pairs against a predicted +0.05; the learned second-attempt depth is 0.09. But continued optimisation itself is a large, qualified gain: CONTINUE minus R-116 +0.104 [+0.086, +0.121] (1,141 vs 982; 183 wins, 24 losses; Holm p 1.7e-29; drops 72 vs 70, bound holds), RETRY minus R-116 +0.107 [+0.090, +0.125]. Sustained successes of 1,536: reference 665 (0.433), R-106 root 838 (0.546), R-116 982 (0.639), CONTINUE 1,141 (0.743), RETRY 1,147 (0.747); drops 76 / 48 / 70 / 72 / 66. CONTINUE minus the reference +0.310 [+0.279, +0.340]. R-116 replicates on new cases (+0.094 over the root, 144 wins, 0 losses). Learned changes: trigger threshold at its upper bound 4.0 rad/s, ramp-out 10.8 steps (bound 10), little-finger depth 0.15, decision step 151; the module triggers in 565 of 1,536 episodes. No deviation; 417,228 starts charged. The CONTINUE finalist (simpler structure) is frozen in config/pen_spin/r118_continue_finalist/; the saved incumbent asset is unchanged.
**Next.** Adopt the simpler structure: the CONTINUE finalist (11 parameters, one decision) is the qualified best controller; freeze it as an asset; installing it as the incumbent is the user's call. Next within the direction: (1) two parameters are on their bounds (threshold 4.0, ramp-out near 10) and training was still improving at update 24, so widen those bounds and continue (a cheap, budget-matched question: does it keep improving?); (2) R-119 (joint adaptation of the residual with the module) is now the main structural question and should be re-triaged from the CONTINUE finalist; (3) re-audit the remaining 26% of failures on this archive before choosing.

Terminal means frozen; the D-072 reference, JOINT and both finalists on twelve fresh 128-case manifests (seeds 5013-5024); five-contrast Holm family. REFUTED: EARLY minus CONTINUE +0.013, 95% [-0.009, +0.035] over 1,536 pairs against a predicted +0.05. Neither finalist improves on JOINT: CONTINUE -0.016 [-0.038, +0.006], EARLY -0.003 [-0.026, +0.020]; neither qualifies. Sustained successes of 1,536: reference 662, JOINT 1,207 (0.786), CONTINUE 1,182, EARLY 1,202; drops 70 / 71 / 74 / 59. The start-up offsets cut early drops from 42 to 29 (the first change there since the R-106 residual) but persistent-slow failures rose from 158 to 174. JOINT has plateaued under ARS continuation at about 0.79-0.81; it remains the best qualified controller (+0.355 [+0.325, +0.385] over D-072 here). No deviation; 414,155 starts charged.
**Next.** JOINT has plateaued under ARS continuation, so further 'more updates' or small additive terms are unlikely to pay; keep the R-121 JOINT finalist as the best controller. The early-drop reduction is real but small (13 episodes of 1,536) and was offset elsewhere. Remaining failures of JOINT here: 158 persistent slow, 75 fast-but-unsteady, 42 early and 29 late drops, 25 off-centre. R-123's precondition is met (neither finalist qualifies), but the direction should now weigh a consolidated replication of the ladder and a hand-off to the user against one more mechanism.

Do not repeat R-102, retune its confirmation cases, reopen cold whole-controller generation, or resume expert cloning. Continue evidence-driven follow-ups within this direction through the existing loop, including its escalation and exhaustion rules.
